### [1] [Explaining GAND: A Resource on Gender-Ambiguous Natural Data & Contrastive Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22546)
*Janiça Hackenbuchner,Jasper Degraeuwe,Arda Tezcan,Joke Daems*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出GAND数据集用于评估机器翻译在性别模糊情境下的性别偏差，并通过特征归因分析揭示上下文中影响性别翻译的源词。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器翻译系统仍存在性别偏见，缺乏自然、性别模糊的基准资源，难以评估和解释系统在缺乏明确性别线索时的翻译行为。

Method: 构建GAND数据集，包含英文源句，设计为自然的性别模糊场景；将其翻译成两种有语法性别的语言，并人工制作对照翻译；使用特征归因技术分析上下文中哪些源词决定目标语言的性别选择。

Result: 实验表明，特定的上下文词汇会显著影响目标语言的性别翻译，揭示了模型在性别歧视背后的语言线索。

Conclusion: GAND提供了评估和解释MT系统性别偏差的有效工具，帮助识别并潜在减轻翻译中的性别刻板印象。

Abstract: Machine translation (MT) systems continue to produce gender-biased translations. In a time where self-expression is paramount, mistranslations based on default behaviour and stereotyping can lead to harm for users of these systems. To better understand how these systems translate gender in the absence of clear gender cues, we need benchmarking resources that reflect gender-ambiguous scenarios in a natural way. To this end, we present GAND, a gender-ambiguous natural data benchmarking resource for MT consisting of English source sentences, specifically designed to analyse the influence of contextual cues on gender in translation. We leverage GAND to conduct an interpretability analysis: we translate a subset of GAND into two grammatical gender languages and extend these with manually crafted contrastive translations. A following feature attribution analysis reveals source words in context that inform the gender translation of an ambiguous referent entity in the target translation.

</details>


### [2] [MioFFAn: an Annotation Software for Formula Formalization with LLM Automation Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22552)
*Nicolas Sibuet,Horacio Saggion,Riccardo Rossi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MioFFAn框架，用于快速标注科学文献中的数学公式并将其形式化为可执行符号代码，支持自定义分类、符号属性以及与大语言模型的部分自动化，展示了人机协同的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的技术科学领域缺乏高质量、真实标注的公式形式化数据集，导致自动将文献中的数学表达式转化为可执行代码的研究受限，需要一种高效、可定制的标注工具来填补数据缺口。

Method: 在MioGatto架构基础上扩展为MioFFAn，提供文档级别的标注界面，支持选择感兴趣的公式、辅助指定符号代码、配置自定义分类和符号属性以及兼容的符号运算符；同时定义一套模块化的自动化子任务，输出格式严格，便于集成大语言模型进行部分自动化并进行迭代评估。

Result: 通过初步实验，展示了在人机协同流程下，MioFFAn能够有效提升标注效率并生成符合标准NLP评估指标的高质量数据，验证了框架的可行性和灵活性。

Conclusion: MioFFAn提供了一个开放、可定制且易于集成自动化的标注平台，为公式形式化数据集的构建提供了实用工具，促进了大语言模型在该任务中的应用与进一步研究。

Abstract: The automatic translation of mathematical expressions in scientific literature into executable symbolic code (a process we refer to as Formula Formalization) is hindered by a severe scarcity of high-quality, ground-truth datasets specialized for technical scientific domains. In this paper, we present MioFFAn, an open-source, document-centric, and customizable framework designed to facilitate rapid annotation for this task. Building upon the MioGatto architecture, we extend existing features to overcome structural limitations and pivot its scope by introducing specific functionalities for Formula Formalization, such as selection of equations of interest and aided symbolic code specification. By allowing users to configure custom taxonomies and properties for identified symbols, and compatible symbolic operators, we ensure the framework is adaptable to diverse specialized scientific fields. Furthermore, MioFFAn is designed to incorporate partial automation via Large Language Models. By defining a modular set of automated sub-tasks with strict output formats, we enable researchers to iteratively refine automation capabilities and evaluate competing strategies using standard NLP metrics. We specify the current automation methodology and perform a preliminary evaluation that demonstrates to efficacy of this human-in-the-loop approach.

</details>


### [3] [Evaluating the Impact of Reviewer Guideline Design on LLM-Based Automated Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22553)
*Haowen Li,Yoichi Ishibashi,Masafumi Oyamada*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究比较官方会议指南与基于大模型生成的模仿审稿人指南对自动化同行评审的影响，发现官方指南最能匹配人工评判，而严格的评分量表反而降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着审稿工作量激增，自动化审稿需求上升；但不同的审稿指导原则可能对自动评审效果产生显著影响，需要系统评估哪类指南更适合作为自动审稿的依据。

Method: 收集官方会议审稿指南和利用高质量人工评审通过大语言模型生成的审稿人模仿指南；在同一数据集上使用自动审稿模型分别依据这些指南进行评审；比较其与人工评审的一致性，并分析严格量表评分对性能的影响。

Result: 官方会议指南下的自动审稿结果与人工判断最为一致；模仿指南整体效果逊于官方指南；强制使用严格的rubric式评分会持续降低自动审稿性能，表明主观、整体评分更有利。

Conclusion: 经过会议实践提炼的官方审稿指南是自动化审稿的最佳参考，过度形式化的评分标准会削弱模型表现，未来自动审稿系统应采用灵活、综合的评估框架。

Abstract: Peer review is an essential process in scientific research, yet the growing workload has made its automation increasingly necessary. In this study, we analyze how different types of reviewer guidelines, such as official conference guidelines and reviewer-imitating ones generated from high-quality human reviews using LLMs, affect automated peer review. Our experiments show that official conference guidelines produce review results most consistent with human judgments, suggesting that evaluation criteria refined through conference practice serve as effective guidance for automated reviewing as well. In contrast, reviewer-imitating guidelines were generally less effective than official conference guidelines. Furthermore, enforcing strict rubric-style scoring consistently degraded performance, highlighting the importance of allowing subjective and holistic scoring.

</details>


### [4] [Learning When to Reason for Text-to-SQL via SFT and DPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22622)
*Soohyuk Jang,Jiheum Yeom,Nohil Park,Sang Hun Kim,Yoonyoung Choi,Kiwook Bae,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AutoThinkSQL 在 Text-to-SQL 任务中引入自动思考机制，使模型对简单查询直接生成 SQL，对复杂查询使用链式思考（CoT），在保持或提升准确率的同时显著降低输出长度和推理时延。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Text-to-SQL 方法普遍采用链式思考（CoT）提升复杂查询的性能，但对大量简单查询而言，强制多步推理导致不必要的计算开销和响应延迟。需要一种能够根据查询难度自适应是否进行推理的方案。

Method: 提出 AutoThinkSQL 框架，在 Supervised Fine‑Tuning (SFT) 与 Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) 两阶段训练中加入自动思考模块。该模块通过学习判断查询难度，决定是直接输出 SQL（跳过 CoT）还是进入深度 CoT 推理。模型在训练时通过偏好学习使决策与查询复杂度对齐。

Result: 在 Qwen3‑Coder‑30B‑A3B 上，AutoThinkSQL 相比仅使用 CoT 的基线在 Spider 与 BIRD 两大基准上均取得一致的性能提升；同时平均输出 token 减少 24.6%（Spider）/18.3%（BIRD），推理时延分别降低 17.1%/11.5%。

Conclusion: AutoThinkSQL 成功实现了查询难度感知的自适应推理，既保持了对复杂查询的高准确率，又显著提升了效率，表明在 Text-to-SQL 任务中可通过动态推理策略兼顾性能与成本。

Abstract: Recent Text-to-SQL methods rely heavily on reasoning-centric paradigms such as Chain-of-Thought (CoT), achieving substantial gains on complex benchmarks at the cost of high inference-time overhead. However, a large fraction of real-world queries are simple lookups or aggregations that can be resolved without multi-step deduction, making forced reasoning wasteful. Thus, we propose AutoThinkSQL, a framework that integrates an auto-thinking mechanism into both Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) on Text-to-SQL. Our approach enables the model to dynamically bypass reasoning for simple queries while invoking deep CoT for complex queries. On Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B, our method achieves consistent gains compared to the best counterpart baseline on both Spider and BIRD benchmarks while simultaneously reducing average output tokens by 24.6% and 18.3%, and average latency by 17.1% and 11.5% compared to CoT-only generation. Further analysis indicates that the model learns to align its reasoning decisions with query difficulty.

</details>


### [5] [Between Suppression and Collapse: Evaluating Narrative Unlearning with LENS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22657)
*Viktoriia Makovska,George Fletcher*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LENS评估框架和SCE评分，检验LLM在不同层级上对特定错误叙事的抑制效果，发现部分检查点能降低叙事复现，但抽象提示可能导致实体恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM能够生成与错误信息相符的叙事框架，现有机器忘记技术能否有效抑制此类叙事尚不明确，需要系统评估方法。

Method: 构建基于情境的LENS评估协议，设定直接、归因、对比、抽象四个抑制层级；在四种近12B多语言指令模型上，对两类来源导向的错误叙事（俄乌战争与北约扩张、美国对台湾的利用/抛弃）进行实验；提出抑制‑崩溃效率（SCE）分数综合衡量抑制效果与输出质量。

Result: 在部分检查点上能够显著降低目标叙事的再现，且抑制效果可在非直接忘记提示下迁移；然而抽象A/B/C提示会导致模型恢复与目标框架相关的真实实体，出现副作用。

Conclusion: LENS是一套有效的诊断工具，可用于报告和指导叙事忘记研究，表明叙事抑制在技术层面可行但仍存实体恢复等潜在问题，需要进一步深入结构化研究。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can reproduce disinformation-aligned narrative frames as plausible explanations, raising the question of whether existing machine-unlearning algorithms can suppress this behavior. We introduce Level-based Evaluation of Narrative Suppression (LENS), a contextualization based evaluation protocol for testing target narrative reproduction across direct, attributed, contrastive, and abstract resistance levels. We evaluate two source-grounded narratives: one framing Russia's war against Ukraine as forced by NATO expansion, and one framing the United States as exploiting or abandoning Taiwan. The experiments cover four near-12B multilingual instruction models: Lapa LLM, Gemma-12B, Qwen-14B, and TAIDE-Gemma.
  We introduce the Suppression-Collapse Efficiency (SCE) score as a checkpoint selection summary that rewards target-narrative suppression while penalizing degraded outputs. Our results shows that selected checkpoints can reduce narrative reproduction and suppression may transfer beyond direct forget prompts. We also report entity recovery as a separate side effect: abstract A/B/C prompts can cause models to recover the real-world actors associated with the target frame after unlearning. These findings demonstrate that LENS is a successful diagnostic protocol for both reporting and guiding the further study of the deeper structure of narrative unlearning.

</details>


### [6] [PatiGonit22K: A Comprehensive Dataset for Solving Complex Bengali MWPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22859)
*Swastika Kundu,Azizul Hakim Fayaz,Tashreef Muhammad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 推出了规模为22,441题的孟加拉语数学文字题数据集PatiGonit22K，提升了低资源语言在数学推理评测中的数据规模与难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有孟加拉语数学文字题资源稀缺，难以支撑自然语言理解与量化推理的研究，需要更大、更复杂的标注数据。

Method: 在原PatiGonit数据集基础上扩充，收集并翻译大量复杂数学题，进行文化适配、注释和数学正确性验证，确保语言一致性与题目质量。

Result: 构建了包含22,441道题、涵盖简单到多步运算的平衡基准，为不同难度层次的数学推理提供评测平台。

Conclusion: PatiGonit22K显著提升了孟加拉语数学文字题的规模与复杂度，为低资源语言的数学推理研究和教育NLP应用提供了更全面的资源。

Abstract: Mathematical Word Problems (MWPs) are an important benchmark for evaluating natural language understanding and quantitative reasoning. Despite recent progress in high resource languages, Bengali remains underexplored due to the limited availability of large scale annotated datasets. In this work, we introduce PatiGonit22K, an expanded Bengali MWP dataset containing 22,441 problems, developed by extending the original PatiGonit dataset with a substantially larger collection of complex mathematical problems. The dataset includes both simple and multi operation equations, providing a balanced benchmark for evaluating mathematical reasoning across different difficulty levels. Each problem is carefully translated, annotated, culturally adapted, and verified to ensure linguistic consistency and mathematical correctness. By increasing both the scale and complexity of Bengali MWPs, PatiGonit22K provides a more comprehensive resource for future research on mathematical reasoning and educational NLP applications in low resource languages.

</details>


### [7] [CHiPS: Character Histograms and Positional Signals for Lightweight Authorship Attribution in Romanian Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22884)
*Sanda-Maria Avram,George C. Ţurcaş*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出轻量级字符级作者归属方法CHiPS，利用字符直方图和傅里叶谱特征，在严格防泄漏的实验中实现约0.93的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无需分词、句法分析或大型预训练模型的前提下，探索仅使用单字符统计和位置谱特征能在罗马尼亚语文本作者归属任务中达到多高的性能，并在严格防泄漏条件下评估其透明性和可解释性。

Method: CHiPS包括两部分：CH‑SVM（基于单字符边缘分布的直方图SVM分类器）和FFT12‑LR（将选定字符和标点映射为冲激序列，提取Fourier/Welch频谱描述并用逻辑回归分类）。此外提供决策层融合CHiPS‑F以及仅基于折叠外预测的Top‑5列表重排序器。整个流程不使用字符n‑gram（n≥2）特征，也不依赖预训练语言模型。

Result: 在ROST闭集划分（10位作者，400篇文件）上，CHiPS‑F取得0.9310的准确率和0.9341的macro‑F1；在更大且有重叠的ROSTories-cleaned数据集（19位作者，1,248篇文件）上，CHiPS‑R得到0.8919准确率和0.8708 macro‑F1。对比使用2‑5字符TF‑IDF SVM的上限（1.0准确率），表明CHiPS在受限特征下仍保持较高性能。

Conclusion: 即使仅依赖单字符直方图和位置谱特征，且在严格防泄漏控制下，CHiPS也能实现接近90%以上的作者归属准确率，证明了透明、轻量特征在作者归属任务中的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: We propose CHiPS, a lightweight character-level authorship attribution method for Romanian texts. All reported experiments are closed-set: the true author is one of the candidate authors in the training data. CHiPS studies two complementary fingerprints of writing style: CH-SVM, a character-histogram classifier based on one-character marginal distributions, and FFT12-LR, a positional-signal classifier that represents selected characters and punctuation classes as impulse trains (binary indicator sequences over character positions) and extracts Fourier/Welch spectral descriptors. We also report CHiPS-F, a leakage-safe decision-level fusion variant, and an optional top-5 listwise reranker trained only on out-of-fold predictions. The method requires no tokenization, syntactic analysis, pretrained language model, or transformer fine-tuning, and it avoids character $n$-gram features with $n \geq 2$ in the histogram component. On a locked grouped ROST split comprising 400 files from 392 source-text groups, written by 10 authors, with source-text-level evaluation and grouped five-fold model selection, CHiPS-F reaches 0.9310 accuracy and 0.9341 macro-F1. A matched but unrestricted character 2--5-gram TF--IDF SVM comparator reaches 1.0000 accuracy and macro-F1 on the same held-out groups, so the contribution is not a claim of best possible classification accuracy. Instead, the experiments ask how far restricted, transparent character evidence can go under strict leakage control. On ROSTories-cleaned, a secondary ROST-overlapping corpus comprising 1,248 files from 1,240 source-text groups, written by 19 authors, the same protocol gives 0.8919 accuracy and 0.8708 macro-F1 for CHiPS-R.

</details>


### [8] [Simple Language Normalization Wins: Cross-Lingual Speaker Verification for the TidyVoice 2026 Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22923)
*Nina Hosseini-Kivanani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过在嵌入空间中使用语言子空间投影（NAP）进行语言归一化，将跨语言说话人验证的EER从约3%降至2.18%，在TidyVoice2026挑战中取得8.40的评分，证明简单的后端归一化可媲美复杂系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨语言不匹配是说话人验证性能下降的主要原因，尤其在没有语言标签的测试场景下，需要有效的语言归一化方法来提升系统鲁棒性。

Method: 在官方SimAM-ResNet34基线模型的嵌入上，利用跨语言同一说话人的差异估计紧凑的语言子空间，并将嵌入投影到该子空间的正交补；随后使用余弦相似度和自适应对称归一化（AS-Norm）进行评分。

Result: 开发集EER从使用余弦的2.97%和AS-Norm的2.70%降至2.18%；在Codabench评测中获得8.40的分数，显著优于未做语言归一化的基线。

Conclusion: 简单的语言子空间投影（NAP）作为后端语言归一化手段，能够显著降低跨语言说话人验证错误率，且在大规模多语言评测中表现出与更复杂系统相当的效果。

Abstract: Cross-lingual mismatch remains a key source of overall degradation in modern speaker verification. The TidyVoice2026 Challenge targets this setting with text-independent verification, comprising 3,666 training and 808 development speakers in 40 languages and 2,200 evaluation speakers in 38 unseen languages, without language labels at test time. Starting from the official SimAM-ResNet34 baseline pretrained on VoxBlink2 and VoxCeleb2 and fine-tuned on TidyVoice, we revisit Nuisance Attribute Projection (NAP) as a simple language-normalization step in the embedding space. We estimate a compact language subspace from cross-language same-speaker differences and project embeddings onto its orthogonal complement before cosine scoring with Adaptive Symmetric score normalization. This reduces development EER from 2.97\% with cosine and 2.70\% with AS-Norm to 2.18\% and yields a Codabench evaluation score of 8.40, showing that simple back-end language normalization can rival more complex systems.

</details>


### [9] [Not All LLM Reasoning is Visible in the Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22925)
*Vatsal Baherwani,Tom Goldstein,Ashwinee Panda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 前沿语言模型在推理时会使用语义无关的填充词，使推理过程在输出中不可见，导致在合成推理任务上准确率提升最高13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型是否将全部推理过程显式体现在输出标记中，评估其潜在的不可见推理风险，以提升AI安全性。

Method: 在三类合成推理任务上对13个前沿模型进行实验，向输入中加入语义无关的填充标记，比较有无填充时的表现；进一步在Claude Opus 4.5上加入隐藏的模算约束测试不可见推理；评估强化学习和监督微调对填充效应的影响。

Result: 多数模型在加入填充词后准确率显著提升，最高提升13%；不同模型对填充词的敏感度不同；Claude Opus 4.5 能在不影响主任务准确率的前提下满足隐藏的模算约束；RL（如Qwen3-235B）对填充内容有偏好，但无论是RL还是SFT都未能在测试时保持填充带来的收益。

Conclusion: 前沿语言模型已经能够在输出中不留下可解释痕迹地进行重要计算，表明不可见推理已在实际模型中出现，对AI安全监控提出了新的挑战。

Abstract: A key question for AI safety is whether a language model expresses all of its reasoning in its output tokens. We demonstrate a concrete failure mode where frontier models exhibit invisible reasoning by leveraging semantically irrelevant filler tokens to improve performance on synthetic reasoning tasks. We evaluate 13 frontier language models across three tasks and find that many models benefit significantly from filler tokens, with accuracy improvements of up to 13 percentage points. The benefit depends on which tokens are used and differs across models. We further show that filler tokens enable Claude Opus 4.5 to satisfy a hidden modular arithmetic constraint without sacrificing accuracy on its primary task, demonstrating that invisible reasoning can serve objectives entirely invisible to CoT monitoring. Reinforcement learning gives Qwen3-235B strong preferences over filler token content, but neither RL nor supervised fine-tuning produces a filler token benefit that persists at test time. Our results indicate that frontier models already perform consequential computation with no interpretable trace in their output tokens.

</details>


### [10] [Toward Automated Detection of Documentation Inconsistencies in Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22954)
*Jian Lu,Panyu Chen,Miriam Treggiari,Robert Blessing,Danyang Zhuo,Chunhua Weng,William W. Stead,Anru R. Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 利用Gemini 2.5系列模型两阶段流水线在3000份MIMIC-IV出院记录中检测内部文档不一致，发现3460条候选问题，覆盖约70%住院，揭示时间推理等导致的模型失效模式，并提出分层本体框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录（EHR）中常出现内部信息不一致，可能危及临床决策和患者安全。缺乏大规模、自动化的检测方法，亟需评估通用大语言模型在真实出院摘要中发现此类错误的能力。

Method: 构建两阶段LLM流水线：第一阶段使用Gemini 2.5 Pro进行开放式候选不一致项抽取；第二阶段用Gemini 2.5 Flash进行基于上下文的验证。对3,000份随机抽取的MIMIC‑IV出院摘要运行流水线，并由临床专家对部分输出进行人工审查。

Result: 流水线共识别出3,460条候选不一致，涉及人口学、过敏、手术、诊断、实验室、药物和护理计划等七大领域，影响69.7%住院。专家审查发现模型在需要时间推理、诊断演变或门诊处方常识的场景下易出现错误。提出了从严格矛盾到模糊歧义的分层本体，并给出每条标记的类别、章节、领域和不一致轴等属性。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了利用通用LLM进行EHR内部不一致检测的初步方法和概念框架，为后续大规模、经验证的电子病历一致性分析奠定了基础。

Abstract: Objective: To characterize the kinds of internal documentation inconsistencies a general-domain large language model (LLM) can surface from real-world discharge summaries, and to identify recurring failure modes that limit reliability at scale.
  Materials and Methods: We applied a two-stage LLM pipeline---open-ended candidate identification (Gemini 2.5 Pro) followed by context-grounded verification (Gemini 2.5 Flash)---to 3,000 randomly sampled MIMIC-IV-Note discharge summaries. A subset of the pipeline output was then reviewed manually by clinical experts.
  Results: Our pipeline surfaced 3,460 candidate inconsistencies, affecting 69.7% of admissions. Representative examples spanned demographics, allergies, procedures, diagnoses, laboratory, medications, and care-planning domains, with direct implications for clinical reasoning or patient safety. Expert review also revealed recurring failure modes that arise when verification requires temporal reasoning, evolving-diagnosis context, or knowledge of outpatient-prescribing conventions the model does not natively possess.
  Discussion: Detection is highly context-dependent: many flagged pairs require anchoring each statement to its source section and clinical domain, then assessing whether the conflict reflects a true contradiction or missing context. We propose a graded ontology spanning strict contradiction and ambiguity, with a schema characterizing each flagged case by category, section, domain, and inconsistency axis.
  Conclusion: This formative study establishes a methodological foundation and conceptual framework to guide subsequent validated, large-scale EHR-inconsistency analysis.

</details>


### [11] [Beyond Direct Answering: Aligning Educational LLMs as Socratic Guides via Heuristic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22996)
*Xiaokun Wang,Siyu Song,Wentao Liu,Xiaodong Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出HeuristicEdu两阶段管线，通过监督热身和GRPO将Qwen2.5-7B对齐为苏格拉底式辅导，使用797条中文儿童科学对话数据和认知深度、好奇心参与、直接性奖励，显著提升脚手架有效性（30%→63.3%）并降低关键词泄漏（30%→13.3%），表明规模不足以自然产生苏格拉底行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在教育场景中倾向直接给出答案，缺乏引导式、递进式的苏格拉底式提问，导致学生主动思考和探究不足，需要方法使模型具备引导式教学能力。

Method: HeuristicEdu包括两阶段：①监督热身阶段使用SocraticEdu数据集（797条多轮中文儿童科学对话）进行基础对齐；②Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)阶段引入启发式奖励函数R_cog（认知深度）、R_eng（好奇心参与）和R_dir（直接性），并加入K_query校正以处理学生自定义术语。评估指标采用Scaffolding Effectiveness (SE)和Conversation Depth (CD)。

Result: 在30个未见问题上，最佳GRPO变体将SE从30.0%提升至63.3%，关键词泄漏率从30.0%降至13.3%。该变体在优化时去除了直接性惩罚，表明显式防泄漏项可能与梯度对齐冲突。未对齐的Qwen-72B基线表现为SE 0%，泄漏率96.7%，说明模型规模本身不足以实现苏格拉底行为。

Conclusion: 通过两阶段对齐和启发式奖励，能够显著提升大模型在教育对话中的引导深度和脚手架有效性，验证了专门的行为对齐比单纯扩大模型规模更关键，为构建苏格拉底式教学AI提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) deployed in educational settings often behave as direct answerers: they disclose target concepts in the opening turn instead of guiding students through progressive inquiry, as Socratic pedagogy prescribes. We present HeuristicEdu, a two-phase pipeline that aligns Qwen2.5-7B toward Socratic tutoring via supervised warm-up and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Training uses SocraticEdu, 797 multi-turn Chinese children's science dialogues reconstructed from a live platform, with a heuristic reward over cognitive depth (R_cog), curiosity engagement (R_eng), and directness (R_dir), together with a K_query correction for student-introduced terms. We introduce Scaffolding Effectiveness (SE) and Conversation Depth (CD) to evaluate outcomes beyond surface fluency. On 30 held-out questions, the best GRPO variant improves SE from 30.0% to 63.3% and lowers keyword leakage from 30.0% to 13.3%. Notably, this best variant omits the directness penalty during optimization, suggesting that explicit anti-leakage terms can conflict with gradient-based behavioral alignment. An unaligned Qwen-72B baseline reaches 0% SE and 96.7% leakage, showing that scale alone does not induce Socratic behavior.

</details>


### [12] [Speech Signals Complement LLMs for Predicting Interpersonal Attraction in Speed Dating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23037)
*Yuriko Kikuchi,Takato Hayashi,Ryusei Kimura,Naoya Inoue,Ryo Ishii,Shogo Okada*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在日语快速约会对话中，结合仅基于文字的 LLM 预测和监督式语音预测，可提升配对吸引力的排名准确度，但对每位参与者的相关性提升仅在特定情境下显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然大型语言模型能够仅凭对话文字预测人际吸引力，但尚不清楚加入语音信息是否能提供额外价值，尤其在实际应用中需要明确语音的增益来源。

Method: 收集日语速配对话数据，使用仅基于转录文本的 LLM 预测伴侣喜好分数；另构建监督学习的语音预测模型。将两者的预测结果线性组合，评估配对的对偶排名准确率以及每位参与者的 Pearson 相关系数，比较不同对话轮次和评分方向的表现。

Result: 两者结合在所有实验条件下显著提升配对排名准确率；但每位参与者的 Pearson r 提升仅在部分对话轮次和评分方向出现，且在多重检验校正后不再显著。提升主要集中在语音预测更准确的参与者身上。

Conclusion: 语音信息在预测人际吸引力时并非普遍有益，而是对特定情境和特定参与者提供互补价值。研究应关注语音增益出现的条件，而非单纯问语音是否有帮助。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can predict interpersonal attraction from conversation transcripts, but it remains unclear what a speech predictor can add beyond transcript-only LLM prediction. Using Japanese speed-dating conversations, we combine predictions from a transcript-only LLM and a supervised speech predictor to estimate participants' reported liking of their partners. We show that speech can complement transcript-only LLM prediction, but that this complementarity is conditional rather than universal. Combining the two predictions significantly improves pairwise ranking accuracy over the transcript-only LLM alone in all evaluated conditions. By contrast, gains in per-participant Pearson $r$ vary across conversation rounds and rating directions, with none significant after correction. Retrospectively, these $r$ gains are concentrated among participants for whom the speech predictor is more accurate. Speech can therefore retain predictive value even when an LLM predicts attraction from transcripts. The relevant question is not simply whether speech helps, but where its complementarity emerges.

</details>


### [13] [ADAGE: A Language-Agnostic Pipeline for Analogical Reasoning Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23058)
*Ahmed Haj Ahmed,Alvin Grissom*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ADAGE管线，利用母语者筛选和大模型生成，构建阿拉伯语、阿姆哈拉语和日语的抽象类比推理基准，发现模型在非英语语言上表现显著下降，文化推理存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多语言推理评估主要依赖将英文基准翻译，导致语言人工痕迹并无法检验文化背景推理能力，亟需无翻译、语言无关的评估方法。

Method: 设计ADAGE：①邀请目标语言的母语者进行概念与类比题目策划；②利用大语言模型在母语者提供的提示下自动生成多样化、具挑战性的抽象类比题；③通过人工过滤和质量控制得到高质量基准。分别为阿拉伯语、阿姆哈拉语和日语构建数据集。

Result: 在14个开源权重模型上进行评测，模型在英文基准上的表现良好，但在三种本地基准上准确率下降12%~52%不等，显示出显著的文化推理差距。

Conclusion: ADAGE提供了一套语言无关、文化根植的类比推理评估框架，揭示了当前模型在跨语言、跨文化推理上的局限，并为后续模型改进和多语言评估提供资源。

Abstract: Multilingual reasoning evaluation overwhelmingly relies on translating English benchmarks, a practice that introduces linguistic artifacts and fails to test culturally-grounded reasoning. We introduce ADAGE (Analogical Difficulty-by-design Assessment for Grounded Evaluation), a language-agnostic pipeline that combines native-speaker curation with LLM-assisted generation to construct challenging, translation-free benchmarks for abstract analogical reasoning. We validate ADAGE by constructing benchmarks for Arabic, Amharic, and Japanese. Evaluating 14 open-weight models, we find a consistent cultural reasoning gap: models that perform well on English proverb reasoning struggle substantially on all three native benchmarks, with accuracy dropping by 12--52 percentage points relative to English. We release the pipeline, all three benchmarks, and the full evaluation suite.

</details>


### [14] [Attention-Guided Layer Selection for Contrastive Decoding in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23067)
*Yusuke Sakai,Natthawut Kertkeidkachorn,Kiyoaki Shirai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于自注意力信息的三种层选择策略（Attention-JSD、Attention-Entropy-Max、Attention-Entropy-Min），在TruthfulQA上超越原始DoLa，尤其在多答案指标上表现显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DoLa 通过对比成熟层和早期层的输出词分布来提升大模型的事实性，但仅依赖词汇分布的散度，可能忽略了内部注意力结构信息，限制了层选择的敏感性。

Method: 利用内部自注意力机制的结构特征设计三种层选择信号：
1) Attention-JSD：计算不同层注意力分布的 Jensen‑Shannon 散度；
2) Attention-Entropy-Max：选择注意力熵最大的层；
3) Attention-Entropy-Min：选择注意力熵最小的层。将这些信号替代 DoLa 的词分布散度进行动态层选择。

Result: 在 TruthfulQA 基准上，三种策略均优于原 DoLa，尤其 Attention-JSD 与 Attention-Entropy-Min 在多答案指标 MC2、MC3 上取得显著提升，表明注意力分布对事实知识的判别更为敏感。

Conclusion: 注意力分布提供了比词汇分布更有效的层选择信号，基于注意力的对比解码能够进一步提升 LLM 的事实性，特别是在需要多答案推理的任务中。

Abstract: Contrastive decoding methods such as DoLa improve the factuality of Large Language Models (LLMs) by contrasting the output distributions of mature and premature layers. However, DoLa's dynamic layer selection relies solely on divergences in output vocabulary distributions. In this work, we propose three attention-guided strategies: Attention-JSD, Attention-Entropy-Max, and Attention-Entropy-Min, which leverage structural information carried by internal self-attention mechanisms as a signal for layer selection. Experimental results on TruthfulQA demonstrate that our strategies, particularly Attention-JSD and Attention-Entropy-Min, consistently outperform the original DoLa. We observe significant gains on multi-answer metrics (MC2 and MC3), suggesting that attention distributions can provide a more sensitive signal for resolving factual knowledge than output vocabulary distributions.

</details>


### [15] [LoRA for Gender-Inclusive Rewriting and Activation Steering for Counter-Narrative Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23083)
*Akhil Rajeev P,Manoj Balaji J*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出在推理阶段通过对Gemma-3-4B-it模型隐藏层激活进行PCA导出的主导方向进行注入，实现性别包容改写和反叙事生成，分别取得80.00%和78.12%的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的性别包容文本改写和反叙事生成多依赖大规模参数微调，计算成本高且难以快速适配；因此需要一种计算高效、无需修改模型权重的控制方法。

Method: 1) 性别包容改写使用参数高效的LoRA微调；2) 反叙事生成在推理时对Gemma-3-4B-it的中间表示注入通过对比隐藏状态激活的主成分（PCA）得到的 steering direction，并结合约束式提示，实现行为引导。

Result: 在LT-EDI 2026共享任务中，性别包容改写得分80.00%，反叙事生成得分78.12%。手工分析揭示了语义漂移、残留偏见泄漏、层敏感性、过度引导和文本退化等失败模式。

Conclusion: 激活层引导是一种轻量级、计算友好的可控生成手段，能够在不更新模型参数的前提下实现社会对齐的语言输出，但仍面临语义保持和偏见残留等挑战，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: Gender-inclusive language generation seeks to transform biased text into inclusive alternatives while preserving semantic meaning and contextual coherence. This paper presents the IHLC system for the LT-EDI 2026 Shared Task, addressing both gender-inclusive rewriting and counter-narrative generation. For gender-inclusive rewriting, we employ parameter-efficient Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) fine-tuning, achieving an official score of 80.00%. Our primary contribution is a compute-efficient inference-time representation engineering approach for counter-narrative generation. We derive a principal steering direction from contrastive hidden-state activations using principal component analysis (PCA) and inject it into the intermediate representations of Gemma-3-4B-it during inference, enabling behavioral steering toward inclusive responses without modifying model weights. Combined with constrained prompting, this approach produces polite and contextually appropriate counter-narratives, achieving an official score of 78.12%. We further present a manual analysis of steering behavior, identifying key failure modes including semantic drift, residual bias leakage, layer sensitivity, over-steering, and text degeneration. Our findings highlight both the practical potential and current limitations of activation steering as a lightweight alternative to parameter updates for controllable and socially aligned language generation.

</details>


### [16] [Interview with Kalle Lyytinen on "Implications of Theories of Language for Information Systems"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23142)
*Kalle J. Lyytinen,Pierre Maier,Paul Ackah Toffey*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Lyytinen回顾40年语言理论对信息系统的影响，访谈中探讨语言核心、生成式AI及大语言模型，并提出未来语言视角的IS研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阐明语言理论自1970年代对信息系统研究的贡献，评估其在大语言模型和生成式AI兴起背景下的价值与挑战。

Method: 通过对Lyytinen本人进行深度访谈，回顾历史文献，结合当前AI技术趋势进行概念性分析与研究议程梳理。

Result: 确认语言是信息系统的核心构件，指出生成式AI提供新的语义建模与交互方式，并提出若干研究议题，如语言模型在IS设计、治理与价值创造中的作用。

Conclusion: 语言视角仍是信息系统研究的关键路径，未来应深化对大语言模型的理论与实证研究，以推动IS学科的创新与实践转化。

Abstract: Over fourty years after the initial publication of "Implications of Theories of Language for Information Systems" in MIS Quaterly, Lyytinen reflects about the origins of his publication and the developments in this area of research over the past decades. In the here presented interview, Lyytinen discusses the linguistic core of information systems also in light of recent trends and developments in the field, especially with regards to large language models and generative AI. Future research directions following a linguistic perspective on Information Systems (IS) research are outlined.

</details>


### [17] [Beyond a Global Norm: Personalizing Toxicity Sensitivity in Language Models Without Retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23175)
*Rares A. C. Diaconescu,Iulia Slanina,Alina Florea,Andrei B. Trache,Miruna E. Coroi,Anne Arzberger,Jie Yang,Enrico Liscio*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 首次对无训练的三阶段推理时干预方法进行比较，针对用户特定的毒性敏感度进行对齐，误差降低28-47%，但在对齐效果、个性化和语言质量之间存在权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毒性语言的感知具有主观性和情境依赖性，传统的全局对齐方法难以满足不同用户的毒性敏感需求，需要评估无需额外训练的对齐技术在用户特定敏感度上的有效性。

Method: 在推理阶段的三个层面进行干预：预解码阶段（提示条件化与重写）、解码中阶段（对 token、logit 与表示进行引导）以及后解码阶段（候选重排序），并基于 PRISM 数据集的毒性敏感度目标进行评估。

Result: 所有干预方法均将对齐误差降低 28%~47%。实验表明，提升对齐效果会牺牲个性化程度或整体语言质量，三者之间形成显著的多目标权衡。

Conclusion: 毒性敏感度对齐是一个多目标问题，现有的训练无关干预方法虽能显著降低误差，但在实现高效对齐、保持个性化和语言流畅性之间仍需权衡，提示未来研究需在多目标优化上进一步探索。

Abstract: Reducing toxicity is often framed as a global alignment problem, yet perceptions of harmful language are subjective and context-dependent. We present the first comparative evaluation of training-free methods for aligning language generation to user-specific toxicity sensitivities across three inference-time intervention stages: pre-decoding (prompt conditioning and rewriting), in-decoding (token, logit, and representation steering), and post-decoding (candidate re-ranking). Evaluated against toxicity sensitivity targets derived from the PRISM dataset, all methods reduce alignment error by 28-47%. However, the results reveal a fundamental trade-off between alignment effectiveness, personalization, and general language quality, showing how toxicity sensitivity alignment is an inherently multi-objective problem.

</details>


### [18] [IndicTalk: A Large-Scale Persona-Based Multilingual Conversational Corpus for Indic Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23242)
*Sahil Deepak Gawande,Mayank Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文推出IndicTalk，一个规模达13.28万条、覆盖9种印地语系语言、包含英印混合（原文和罗马化）对话的多轮对话数据集，利用多语言大模型自动生成并验证，展示了高流畅性和自然的代码混合特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话资源在多语言、尤其是印地语系语言的代码混合场景极度匮乏，难以支撑对这些语言的对话系统研发；而实际使用中，使用者常在英语与母语之间切换，需有相应的数据。

Method: 构建全自动流水线：①以真实新闻事件为 grounding；②基于多语言 LLM 在 persona 条件下生成多轮对话；③对生成的对话进行自动质量检测（流畅性、一致性、代码混合比例等），并保留符合标准的样本。

Result: 得到 13.28 万条、跨 18 种语言变体（9 种语言）的代码混合对话，经过语言学、自动指标和人工评审验证，数据在流畅性、连贯性和代码混合自然度上均表现优异，且兼容原生脚本和罗马化两种形式。

Conclusion: IndicTalk 为低资源印地语系语言的多语言对话系统提供了大规模、高质量的训练与评估基准，推动了该领域的研究与应用，作者将公开数据以促进社区发展。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have transformed conversational AI, yet high-quality multilingual code-mixed dialogue resources remain scarce, particularly for Indic languages where speakers naturally alternate between English and their native language in both native-script and Romanized forms. We present IndicTalk, one of the largest multilingual Indic code-mixed conversational corpora, comprising over 13,28,604 event-grounded multi-turn conversations across 18 language varieties covering 9 Indic languages. The corpus is generated through a fully automated pipeline that combines real-world news grounding, persona-conditioned dialogue generation using multilingual LLMs, and automatic quality validation. Extensive linguistic, automatic, and human evaluations demonstrate that IndicTalk produces fluent, coherent, and naturally code-mixed conversations across both script variants. We will release IndicTalk to support the development and evaluation of multilingual conversational AI for underrepresented Indic languages. The dataset is available at: https://huggingface.co/datasets/LingoIITGN/IndicTalk .

</details>


### [19] [Co-Evolving Graph and Text Memory for Training-Free Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23278)
*Hieu Man,Thien Huu Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Co-E是一种无需训练、同步双向图文工作记忆的多跳问答系统，通过循环同步文本记忆与图记忆，实现检索与生成的协同提升，在六个基准上超越同类无训练基线并接近有训练的大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多跳问答方法要么只利用图或文本，缺乏两者的协同；图增强检索、KGQA和记忆体方法各自未能在推理过程中持续对齐图谱与文本信息，导致证据整合不足。

Method: 提出Co-E系统，采用训练无关的双向同步工作记忆：①文本记忆收集检索到的段落；②从文本中抽取关系三元组更新图记忆；③将图记忆中的事实注入生成上下文。该同步循环在每一步检索和生成前后进行，使两种记忆相互强化。

Result: 在六个多跳问答基准上，Co-E相较于同类无训练、开放骨干基线有显著提升，并且在性能上可与使用更大模型或需要训练的系统竞争。

Conclusion: 通过保持并同步图文双向记忆，Co-E实现了高效的证据整合，证明在多跳问答任务中，训练自由的图文协同机制是可行且有效的。

Abstract: Multi-hop question answering requires coordinating relational and textual evidence across reasoning steps, a combination neither a text corpus nor a knowledge graph can supply alone. Prior work often emphasizes only part of this loop: graph-augmented RAG retrieves from a pre-built or query-updated graph, KGQA systems search within topic-centered subgraphs, and memory-augmented agents maintain evolving memories without continuously reconciling graph memory with textual context. We propose Co-E, a training-free system built around synchronized bidirectional graph-text working memory. A synchronization cycle consolidates textual memory, extracts relational triples into graph memory, and injects graph facts back into the generation context. Because both memories are maintained, they shape subsequent retrieval and generation. Evaluated on six multi-hop QA benchmarks, Co-E improves over comparable training-free open-backbone baselines and is competitive with larger or trained systems.

</details>


### [20] [BHARATI: Morphology-Aware Tokenizers for Classical Indian Languages with Subword Fertility Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23319)
*Poornima Kumaresan,Pavithra Muruganantham,Lakshmi Rajendran,Santhosh Sivasubramani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 BHARATI，一套针对七种印欧语言（含古典印地语系）的 SentencePiece BPE 分词器，经过三代迭代后实现原生子词覆盖，显著降低技术术语的子词数和序列长度，提升下游模型的有效上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 BPE、SentencePiece 等子词分词器主要在现代语言语料上训练，面对梵语、泰米尔语等古典印地语系语言的黏着形态、沙迪（音变）以及特定领域词汇时，分词效率低下，导致序列过长，限制语言模型的上下文利用。

Method: 构建 781 MB 平衡语料，覆盖英语、印地语、梵语、泰米尔语、泰卢固语、卡纳达语、马拉雅拉姆语。基于 SentencePiece 训练 BPE 分词器，分三版迭代：v1 仅英文+梵语（泰米尔字节回退错误），v2 加入四种语言并为南部语言提供字节级回退，v3 完整七语言原生子词覆盖（词表 32k）。通过子词繁殖率和保留术语单词率评估，并在 490 条 IKS 领域测试集上测序列长度。

Result: v3 在 IKS 专业术语上平均 2.6 个子词，显著低于 GPT-2 分词器的 5.25 和多语言 SentencePiece 基线的 3.75；在六种印地语系语言的测试句子上，序列长度比 GPT-2/字节级编码缩短约 90%，比 mBART‑50 基线缩短约 25%。这些压缩直接提升了下游模型的有效上下文长度。

Conclusion: BHARATI 系列分词器通过针对古典印地语系语言的专门训练，实现了高效子词表示，显著降低序列长度，为后续的语言模型提供更大的上下文空间。模型、训练脚本及评测基准已开源，促进该领域的进一步研究与应用。

Abstract: Standard subword tokenization algorithms such as Byte-Pair Encoding (BPE) and SentencePiece are trained predominantly on modern language corpora and produce inefficient segmentations when applied to classical Indian languages. Sanskrit, Tamil, and other classical Indic languages exhibit agglutinative morphology, productive sandhi (phonological fusion at word boundaries), and domain-specific vocabularies absent from general-purpose training data. This paper presents BHARATI, a set of SentencePiece BPE tokenizers trained on a balanced 781 MB corpus spanning seven languages (English, Hindi, Sanskrit, Tamil, Telugu, Kannada, and Malayalam) with native script support for all languages. We describe three successive tokenizer versions: v1 (English and Sanskrit only, with broken byte-fallback for Tamil), v2 (four-language support with byte-level fallback for southern languages), and v3 (full seven-language native subword coverage). Subword fertility analysis demonstrates that v3 averages 2.6 tokens per Indian Knowledge System (IKS) technical term, compared to 5.25 tokens per term with GPT-2's tokenizer and 3.75 tokens with the multilingual SentencePiece baseline, with the largest gains on a set of reserved IKS terms that are represented as single tokens by construction. On a held-out test set of 490 IKS-domain sentences (70 per language across seven languages, released with the measurement script), v3 reduces sequence length by roughly 90% relative to GPT-2 and byte-level encoding (which lack native Indic subwords) and by approximately 25% relative to the mBART-50 multilingual baseline, averaged across the six Indic languages, directly translating to increased effective context length for downstream language models. The tokenizer models (32,000 vocabulary), training scripts, and evaluation benchmarks are released under open licenses.

</details>


### [21] [IKS-Instruct: A 24,000-Example Multilingual Dataset for Teaching Language Models Indian Knowledge Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23322)
*Shwetha Singaravelu,Gayathri Muruganantham,Lakshmi Rajendran,Santhosh Sivasubramani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了 IKS-Instruct 数据集，包含 24,795 条多语言（7 种语言）指令-响应对，覆盖印度知识体系的 41 种教学技术，并与 CBSE 6‑12 年级课程对齐。对 7B 模型进行微调后，在 12 维度评估中取得 6.39 的中位分，接近通用模型 Nemotron‑Nano（6.54），且成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令微调数据集主要是英文通用知识，缺乏对专业教育领域，尤其是印度本土知识体系（IKS）的覆盖。为提升大语言模型在多语言教育场景下的表现，需要构建针对 IKS 的高质量指令数据。

Method: 收集六类来源：古典文本（如《博伽梵歌》《蒂鲁克尔》等）、课程对齐的教学模板、吠陀数学经文示例、双语指令对、技术驱动的多轮对话、跨传统比较分析。基于这些来源生成 24,795 条指令‑响应对，覆盖七种语言。采用多评审框架，12 维度（技术忠实度、教学质量、事实准确性、文化深度等）进行质量评估，使用 5 位评审的中位聚合，对 1,201 条分层抽样样本进行打分。

Result: 对 7B 模型进行 IKS-Instruct 微调后，在多评审测试中获得中位分 6.39，距离强基准模型 Nemotron‑Nano（6.54）仅 0.15，且部署成本显著降低。未微调的基模型在 IKS 相关维度几乎得分为零。实验还发现，数据质量提升并非单调提升模型性能，呈现出非线性关系。

Conclusion: IKS-Instruct 数据集成功填补了指令微调在印度本土教育领域的空白，证明了在多语言、文化特定教学任务上，针对性微调能够显著提升小模型的表现，且成本效益高。未来工作可进一步探索数据质量与模型性能的非线性关系以及更广泛的跨文化教育应用。

Abstract: Instruction tuning has become the standard method for adapting large language models to follow human intent, yet existing instruction datasets are dominated by English-language general-knowledge tasks and lack coverage of specialized pedagogical domains. This paper presents IKS-Instruct, a dataset of 24,795 instruction-response pairs for teaching language models to deliver educational content grounded in Indian Knowledge Systems (IKS). The dataset spans seven languages (English, Hindi, Sanskrit, Tamil, Telugu, Kannada, and Malayalam), covers 41 pedagogical techniques from the Vedic oral and mathematical traditions, and is aligned with the Central Board of Secondary Education (CBSE) curriculum for classes 6 through 12. The pairs are derived from six source types: classical text corpora (Bhagavad Gita, Thirukkural, Sangam literature, Vedic texts), curriculum-aligned pedagogical templates, Vedic mathematical sutra demonstrations, bilingual instruction pairs, technique-grounded multi-turn dialogues, and cross-tradition comparative analyses. Quality is assessed through a multi-judge evaluation framework in which independent language models score responses on 12 dimensions including technique fidelity, pedagogical quality, factual accuracy, and IKS cultural depth. Under a uniform five-judge external panel (median aggregation over 1,201 stratified items), the strongest IKS-Instruct fine-tune of a compact 7B model reaches a median judge score of 6.39, within 0.15 of a strong general-purpose reference model (Nemotron-Nano at 6.54) at a fraction of its deployment cost, while the base model without IKS fine-tuning scores near zero on the IKS-specific dimensions. Model quality does not increase monotonically with data curation, a result we report together with the corresponding data-quality gains.

</details>


### [22] [BERT-based Models vs. Large Language Models for Low-Resource Named Entity Recognition: A Comparative Study on Marathi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23344)
*Hariom Ingle,Ronit Ghode,Ishwari Gondkar,Jidnyasa Harad,Raviraj Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在马拉地低资源NER任务中，微调MahaBERT‑v2显著优于通用大模型，F1达0.88‑0.91，远超基线0.8843和LLM 0.57‑0.69。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言如马拉地缺乏标注数据，且语言结构复杂，导致NER困难；需评估专用模型与通用大语言模型在此任务上的效果。

Method: 将MahaBERT‑v2在不同版本的MahaNER数据集上进行微调，并与已有MahaNER基线以及Gemini、LLaMA‑3.3‑70B、Gemma等通用LLM进行系统对比，使用精确率、召回率和F1分数评估。

Result: 微调后的MahaBERT模型持续领先，F1在0.88‑0.91之间，超过基线模型0.8843，并显著高于LLM方案（F1 0.57‑0.69）。

Conclusion: 在马拉地NER等低资源语言任务中，针对语言和领域的专用模型仍优于通用大模型，强调了专门架构在低资源处理中的重要性。

Abstract: Named Entity Recognition (NER) for low-resource languages such as Marathi remains a challenging task due to limited annotated resources and linguistic complexity. Although recent Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong performance across a wide range of natural language processing tasks, their effectiveness for language-specific NER in low-resource settings remains uncertain. In this study, we fine-tune MahaBERT-v2 on different variants of the MahaNER dataset and systematically compare the performance of these models with an existing MahaNER baseline and prominent general-purpose LLMs, including Gemini, LLaMA-3.3-70B, and Gemma models. All models are evaluated on a Marathi NER test dataset using standard metrics of precision, recall, and F1-score. The experimental results show that the fine-tuned MahaBERT-based models consistently outperform both the baseline and all evaluated LLMs, with the fine-tuned models achieving F1-scores ranging from 0.88 to 0.91, surpassing the existing MahaNER model (0.8843) and significantly exceeding the performance of LLM-based approaches, whose F1-scores range from 0.57 to 0.69. These findings demonstrate that task-specific, language-focused models trained on domain-relevant data remain more effective than general-purpose LLMs for Marathi NER, highlighting the continued importance of specialized architectures for low-resource language processing.

</details>


### [23] [Joint Optimization for Greedy Longest-match Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23362)
*Adhiraj Singh,Deepanshu Mody,Ghina Al Shdaifat,Hamza Alshamy,Adam Wiemerslage,Varshini Reddy,Craig W. Schmidt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: JOLT 通过整数规划在词表学习阶段对齐最长匹配解码，使得在 32k/64k 词表下相较 BPE 可减少最多 0.78% 的 token 数，且接近最优压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的子词词表（如 BPE）是基于贪心启发式构造的，未针对实际部署时使用的最长匹配解码进行优化，导致压缩率仍有提升空间。

Method: 提出 Joint Optimization for Greedy Longest-Match Tokenization (JOLT)，将词表选择与分词决策建模为整数规划，并加入贪心一致性约束保证训练得到的分词与最长匹配解码完全一致。通过线性规划松弛求解，并对未解决的 pretokens 引入高阶分词，仅在必要时提升精度，最终得到几乎整数的解。

Result: 实验在四种训练范围、词表规模 32k 与 64k 上进行。LP 松弛的解与下界误差仅 0.008–0.176%。BPE 已接近最优，仅差 1–2%。JOLT 缩小了剩余差距的 89.6–99.4%，在验证集上相较 BPE 最多减少 0.78% 的 token 数，且随训练范围增大效果提升。

Conclusion: 将词表学习与实际最长匹配解码对齐的 JOLT 能在几乎最优的压缩率下显著超越传统 BPE，证明了推理对齐的词表优化能够充分利用 BPE 余下的有限压缩空间，并提供近似最优性的理论证书。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that subword vocabularies can be trained to optimize compression for a specific inference rule rather than relying on greedy heuristics such as Byte Pair Encoding (BPE). We extend this approach to greedy left-to-right longest-match decoding, the fast and widely used inference rule underlying WordPiece. We introduce Joint Optimization for Greedy Longest-Match Tokenization (JOLT), which formulates vocabulary learning as an integer program over vocabulary-selection and segmentation-choice variables. Greedy-consistency constraints ensure that each optimized segmentation exactly matches the segmentation produced by longest-match decoding under the selected vocabulary, aligning the training objective with deployment-time tokenization. To scale the optimization, we solve a linear programming relaxation and selectively introduce higher-order segmentations only for unresolved pretokens. The resulting relaxation is nearly integral: rounded solutions fall within 0.008 - 0.176 % of the LP lower bound on the training scope. The bound also shows that BPE is already within 1 - 2 % of the best achievable compression under greedy longest-match decoding, while JOLT closes 89.6 - 99.4 % of the remaining gap. On held-out validation data across four training scopes and vocabulary sizes of 32,000 and 64,000, JOLT produces up to 0.78 % fewer tokens than BPE, with improvements generally increasing as the training scope grows. These results demonstrate that inference-aligned vocabulary optimization can recover most of the limited compression headroom left by BPE while providing a certificate of near-optimality.

</details>


### [24] [When Activation Oracles Learn Not to Read: Concept-Specific Blind Spots in Fine-Tuned Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23379)
*Tobias Bersia,Tatiana Gaintseva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 激活预言机（AO）在读取模型内部隐藏概念时，可能出现选择性失效的现象，即使该概念在模型内部仍可解码，导致解释性接口的可靠性受质疑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究基于语言模型的激活预言机是否真的能够中立、准确地读取模型内部信息，尤其在模型被刻意隐藏某些概念的情况下，评估其作为解释性工具的可靠性。

Method: 构建“禁忌词猜测”实验：先对主体模型进行微调，使其内部使用隐藏概念但不在输出中直接透露；随后训练激活预言机读取该模型的内部激活，并分析其在恢复隐藏概念上的表现，辅以LogitLens和层剥离等技术检查读取路径。

Result: 发现微调后的激活预言机并未成为专门的读取器，反而表现为概念特定的“反读取器”：在自身训练期间持续出现对隐藏概念的恢复失败。该失败并非因为概念在主体或预言机表征中缺失，而是读取路径本身出现了选择性失效。

Conclusion: 行为泄漏、表征可解码性与激活预言机的可言语化能力可能相互脱钩，提示基于学习的解释性接口在可靠性上存在潜在风险，需要进一步研究其读取机制和防止选择性失效的策略。

Abstract: Activation Oracles (AOs) are language models trained to answer natural-language questions about another model's internal activations. They offer a flexible interface for reading hidden information from model states, especially when relevant information is internally represented but absent or incomplete in visible behavior. However, AOs are themselves learned systems: their answers are shaped by training data, objectives, and learned reporting behavior, rather than being neutral readouts of represented information. We study this in a controlled Taboo Word Guessing setting, where subject models are fine-tuned to internally use a hidden concept while avoiding direct disclosure. Contrary to the expectation that an AO trained on such a subject becomes a specialist reader, we find that fine-tuned AOs can become concept-specific anti-readers: they selectively fail to recover the concept persistently present during their own training. This failure is not simply explained by absence of the concept from the subject or oracle representations: the target remains decodable inside the oracle, while LogitLens and layer-ablation analyses indicate that the failure arises in the AO readout pathway. Our results show that behavioral leakage, representation-level decodability, and AO-verbalizability can come apart, raising a reliability concern for learned interpretability interfaces.

</details>


### [25] [LA-RL: Label-Aware Self-Reflection for Reinforcement Learning in Information Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23420)
*Xiao You,Tianwei Yan,Zixu Shan,Longyu Du,Shan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LA-RL框架，通过在信息抽取任务中加入标签感知的自我反思与强化学习，实现对抽取结果的诊断与修正，显著提升NER、关系抽取和事件抽取的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于反思的纠错方法难以对结构化抽取输出进行精准定位，无法区分缺失跨度、标签错误、边界不匹配、关系类型错误或论元顺序颠倒等具体错误，导致纠错效果有限。

Method: LA-RL是一种结果监督的框架：同一模型先生成抽取结果，再预测任务特定的错误标签（诊断标签），随后在诊断条件下对输出进行修正。训练分为两阶段：①使用标注模型生成的诊断数据进行冷启动监督微调；②通过两轮GRPO（Goal‑oriented Reinforcement POlicy）强化学习，奖励最终抽取质量、格式合法性以及首次抽取的正确性，无需额外过程奖励模型。

Result: 在多个IE任务上均取得显著提升：SciER关系抽取平均F1提升6.83，跨域关系抽取提升约20 F1，DuEE1.0事件抽取中触发词F1提升14.80，论元F1提升17.50。对比SFT同骨干模型，均实现一致性增益。

Conclusion: 标签感知的反思结构对不同IE任务具有差异化需求：在关系抽取中强约束有助于提升纠错效果，而在命名实体识别尤其是领域迁移场景下，较宽松的修正策略更为有效。LA-RL展示了将诊断标签与强化学习结合的可行性，为结构化IE的自我纠错提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models show strong promise for information extraction (IE), but existing reflection-based correction methods are often misaligned with structured extraction outputs. Free-form self-reflection can flag an error, yet it rarely identifies whether the failure is a missing span, wrong label, boundary mismatch, invalid relation type, or reversed argument order. We introduce LA-RL (Label-Aware Reflective Reinforcement Learning), an outcome-supervised framework that guides IE self-correction with task-grounded diagnostic labels. A single backbone first predicts an extraction, diagnoses task-specific error labels, and then revises its output conditioned on the diagnosis. Training starts from diagnostic data labeled by an annotation model for cold-start supervised fine-tuning and proceeds through two GRPO stages that reward final extraction quality, format validity, and first-pass correctness, without a process reward model. Experiments on named entity recognition, relation extraction, and event extraction show consistent same-backbone gains over SFT, including 6.83 average F1 on SciER relation extraction, about 20 F1 on out-of-distribution relation extraction, and 14.80 trigger F1 plus 17.50 argument F1 on DuEE1.0. Ablations show that reflection structure is task-sensitive: stronger constraints benefit relation extraction, whereas named entity recognition needs less restrictive correction under domain shift.

</details>


### [26] [Reasoning or Memorization: Can LLMs Understand and Generate Chinese Xiehouyu Riddles?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23440)
*Hai Hu,Siyuan Song,Chongtian Shao,Kejia Zhang,Tianjian Zhu,Xiaojing Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过中文歇后语游戏评估大模型的推理与记忆，发现中文模型在低频歇后语上记忆更好，但在创造新歇后语方面仍逊于人类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为检验大语言模型在中文文化特有任务中的推理能力，并避免数据泄漏对评估的影响，作者构建了全新歇后语并设计多种评测方式。

Method: 使用三类任务：① 多项选择题（MCQ），比较已存在低频歇后语与全新歇后语的准确率差 Δₐ𝚌𝚌 以衡量记忆；② 自由解释生成，评估模型对歇后语的理解；③ 歇后语创作任务，比较模型与人类创作的质量评分。实验覆盖中文前沿模型和以英语为主的模型。

Result: 本土中文模型的 Δₐ𝚌𝚌 平均为 23.6%，显著高于英语中心模型的 5.1%，说明中文模型在低频歇后语上记忆更强。对全新歇后语，Gemini 3.1 Pro 的准确率达 92.6%，比人类高出约 24%。然而在歇后语创作任务中，模型生成的作品评分远低于人类。

Conclusion: 大模型在中文歇后语的记忆表现优于跨语言模型，但其创造力仍不足，提示对大模型推理能力的评价需考虑数据污染问题，且在语言创作类任务上仍落后于人类专家。

Abstract: In this paper, we push the boundary of LLM reasoning by testing them in a Chinese language game, xiehouyu, with novel xiehouyu created by linguists that had not existed before to avoid data contamination. We use multiple-choice questions (MCQ), free-form explanation generation, and new xiehouyu creation to evaluate LLMs' ability to understand and create xiehouyu. In MCQ, we use the delta of accuracy ($Δ_{acc}$) between existing but low-frequency xiehouyu and novel ones as an index for memorization. $Δ_{acc}$ for native speakers is very low, suggesting similar processing mechanisms. However, we found that frontier Chinese models have on average a $Δ_{acc}$ of 23.6\%, while English-centric models tested have a mean $Δ_{acc}$ of 5.1\%, suggesting that frontier Chinese models are likely trained with much larger Chinese data, thus memorizing more low-frequency xiehouyu. For novel xiehouyu, Gemini 3.1 Pro demonstrated remarkable ability with acc 92.6, which is 24\% higher than human accuracy. In xiehouyu creation, those created by LLMs receive much worse ratings than those by humans. These results suggest that claims about the reasoning abilities of LLMs may need careful re-examination considering the data contamination issue, and that LLMs' creativity in language-related tasks may still be behind human experts, at least in Chinese xiehouyu.

</details>


### [27] [Do LLM Debates Repeat Arguments Differently Across Languages?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23442)
*Huiqian Lai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，在多语言辩论中，中文的先前论点相似度显著高于英文，说明中文辩论更倾向于重复已有论点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 LLM 辩论评估仅关注最终答案，忽视了辩论过程中的论点发展与重复情况；因此需要一种度量来捕捉论点的时间演化。

Method: 提出“先前论点相似度”（prior-argument similarity）指标，使用多语言嵌入模型比较同一辩论中后续论点单元与之前单元的相似度；在八轮、71个议题、六种语言、四种模型代理的受控实验中进行评估，并通过回归调整、不同度量变体、抽取长度控制、二次抽取子集及交叉编码器重打分等多种稳健性检验。

Result: 中文在所有实验设置下均表现出相对于英文的正向差距（gap），该差距在不同代理、轮次、度量和控制条件下均保持；人工校准显示整体对齐较弱，但高相似度尾部富含实质性重复。多样性感知提示能够降低各语言的相似度，但未显著缩小中英差距。

Conclusion: 多语言辩论评估应加入论点随时间的演化度量，并在报告时同时给出平均相似度和语言间差距，以更全面地衡量模型的论证能力和潜在偏差。

Abstract: LLM debate is usually evaluated by final answers, but transcripts also reveal whether later turns develop new argumentative content or return to earlier claims in new wording. We study this process with \textit{prior-argument similarity}, an aggregate diagnostic comparing extracted argument units with earlier units in the same debate. In controlled eight-turn debates over 71 motions, six languages, and four model agents, Chinese is the only tested language with a consistently positive gap relative to English across three multilingual embedding models. The gap persists across agents, turn positions, regression adjustment, metric variants, extraction-length controls, a second-extractor subset, and cross-encoder tail rescoring. Manual calibration shows weak item-level alignment but a high-similarity tail enriched for substantive repetition. A diversity-aware prompt lowers prior-argument similarity across languages, yet does not significantly narrow the Chinese--English gap. These findings suggest that multilingual debate evaluation should measure argumentative development over time and report mitigation effects in both average and gap terms.

</details>


### [28] [Do Small Models Use the Law You Give Them? Context-Injected Fine-Tuning for Legal QA in Bangladesh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23446)
*Moniruzzaman Mahadi,Abrar Mohammed Tanzim Alam,Sayma Siddika Monalisa,Mir Mohammad Asif Abdullah,Swakkhar Shatabda,Md Adnan Arefeen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过在2,165条孟加拉语法律问答数据上微调不同规模的Qwen3.5模型，发现0.8B模型在英文法考中显著提升（得分从2提升至34），而4B模型未见整体提升；微调显著降低答案语言漂移，表明检索质量并非唯一瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小规模语言模型在接收到相关法律条文后仍可能给出错误答案，研究者希望探究在法律问答任务中，针对法条进行微调是否能提升模型对检索到法律的利用效果，从而改善答题准确性。

Method: 收集并整理了来自六部孟加拉法律及三部附表的2,165条双语（孟加拉语/机器翻译英文）问答记录。对Qwen3.5模型的0.8B、2B、4B三个规模进行微调。评估使用2022、2023年孟加拉律师协会考试题，分别以孟加拉语和英文（机器翻译）形式进行，无检索、BM25或FAISS检索，仅通过三次随机种子运行的严格一致性评分进行比较。采用配对统计检验评估得分提升，并分析答案语言漂移情况。

Result: 0.8B模型在2022年英文FAISS条件下得分从2提升至34，且在0.8B和2B规模上提升在配对检验中显著；4B模型整体无显著提升，孟加拉语表现提升但英文多项条件退化。微调后答案从孟加拉语漂移至英文的比例从44.0%–53.2%降至0.2%–0.7%，统计显著(p<.001)。

Conclusion: 针对法律条文的微调能够显著提升小规模模型在法律问答中的表现，尤其在降低语言漂移方面效果明显，但检索质量并非唯一限制因素；更大模型未必带来整体提升，说明模型规模、语言使用和法条利用方式之间存在复杂交互。数据集已公开供社区使用。

Abstract: A small language model can receive the governing statutory provision and still answer incorrectly. We test whether fine-tuning on examples containing relevant law improves later use of retrieved law. We curate 2{,}165 bilingual QA records from six Bangladeshi acts and three schedules, then fine-tune Qwen3.5 at 0.8B, 2B, and 4B. Evaluation uses the 2022 and 2023 Bangladesh Bar Council exams in Bangla and machine-translated English, with no retrieval, BM25, or FAISS, scored by strict consistency over three seeded runs. At 0.8B, fine-tuning raises the 2022 English FAISS score from 2 to 34 of 100. Gains at 0.8B and 2B survive paired testing, but the 4B model has no detectable net gain: Bangla improves while several English conditions regress. Fine-tuning also reduces answers that drift from Bangla into mostly English from 44.0--53.2\% to 0.2--0.7\%, with adjusted $p<.001$ at every scale. Retrieval quality is therefore not the only bottleneck. Small bilingual legal models also differ in how they use supplied law and whether they answer in the requested language. The dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/momahadi/bangladesh-legal-qa-dataset.

</details>


### [29] [Two Regimes of Chain-of-Thought Unfaithfulness: Behavioral Detection Fails Where Models Are Wrong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23458)
*Suramya R. Angdembay,Dikshant Aryal,Nick Rahimi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，链式思考（CoT）解释的可信度主要取决于答案是否正确；仅凭答案错误即可检测大部分不可信的CoT，其他行为信号在正确答案上有一定区分能力，但在错误答案上几乎无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际监督中，需要确保模型给出的CoT解释是真实反映其推理过程的，而不是事后编造的解释。作者希望评估现有行为检测方法在识别不可信CoT方面的有效性，并探究答案正确性在其中的作用。

Method: 作者使用FaithCoT-Bench的人类标注数据，对四种大模型的行为信号进行黑箱检测。通过将样本按答案正确性分层，比较不同信号（包括答案错误率、步骤移除指标、线性探针等）的AUROC表现，并分析信号在不同模型和条件下的转移能力。

Result: 1) 仅凭答案错误（oracle）即可达到AUROC 0.696，显著优于所有专门设计的检测信号。2) 在正确答案上，行为信号的AUROC在0.63-0.67之间，能适度区分可信与事后推理。3) 在错误答案上，所有测试信号均未超过随机水平。4) 步骤移除指标与人工标注呈负相关。5) 线性探针在Llama-3.1-8B的错误答案 regime 和 Qwen-2.5-7B的正确答案 regime 中有效，但未发现跨 regime 的共享方向。6) 指令式的先答后推理痕迹在七个模型间不具迁移性，而提示诱导的未言明答案翻转在特定模型和来源下可迁移。7) 纠正了基准标签语义与文档描述的不一致。

Conclusion: 答案正确性是判断CoT解释可信度的关键因素；在错误答案上现有行为检测手段几乎失效，说明需要新的方法或利用答案错误本身作为强信号。与此同时，现有指标如步骤移除可能误导评估，需重新设计以匹配人类标注。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) explanations support oversight only if they are faithful: the stated reasoning must actually produce the answer. Auditing black-box (behavioral) detection of unfaithful CoT against FaithCoT-Bench's human annotations, we find answer correctness structures the problem at every level. Answer incorrectness alone (an oracle diagnostic, not a deployable detector) outperforms every purpose-built signal (AUROC 0.696), because 69% of annotated unfaithfulness occurs on incorrect answers. Stratifying by correctness splits detection into two regimes: on correct answers, behavioral signals moderately separate faithful from post-hoc reasoning (0.63-0.67); on incorrect answers, where most unfaithfulness lives, no tested signal is detectably above chance (replicated on all four models for benchmark-wide signals). The standard step-removal metric anti-correlates with human labels; this inversion reproduces on the benchmark's released scores and on hint-dependent counterfactually labeled traces. Linear probes decode the behaviorally blind regime in Llama-3.1-8B and the correct-answer regime in Qwen-2.5-7B, with no shared, positively aligned direction detected across regimes; instructed answer-first traces (7 models) transfer to neither annotated regime, while hint-induced unverbalized answer flips do, in model- and source-dependent settings. We also independently verify and resolve a documentation-data mismatch in the benchmark's label semantics.

</details>


### [30] [Mwando: Leveraging AI to Preserve and Teach shiKomori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23481)
*Naira Abdou Mohamed,Haidar Nassur Said Ali,Mohamed Hazra,Naoufal Mohamed Soibira,Roushnaty Ali Yamani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Mwando 是面向科摩罗群岛语言 shiKomori 的虚拟教育助理，支持四种方言，利用向量检索、知识图谱和网页搜索的多代理架构提供词汇查询和语法解释，实验表明在 500 条查询上表现良好，为低资源语言的 AI 教育工具提供了示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: shiKomori 作为科摩罗群岛的本土语言，资源稀缺且缺乏数字化教学支持，亟需技术手段帮助语言教学与保存，尤其是覆盖其四大方言的系统。

Method: 构建包含短语、谚语、词典和语法教材的知识库；采用多代理体系：向量检索用于相似句子匹配，知识图谱提供结构化语义关联，若检索失败则回退至网页搜索；系统能够根据上下文生成针对词汇和语法的解释。

Result: 在 500 条人工设计的查询上，Mwando 在词汇查找和语法解释任务上取得高准确率和满意度；案例研究展示了系统在实际教学情境中的可用性及其在方言覆盖、上下文理解方面的局限。

Conclusion: Mwando 证明了面向低资源语言的 AI 教育助理的可行性，为 shiKomori 的数字化保存提供了初步技术方案，并为其他类似语言的教育工具开发提供了可复制的框架。

Abstract: This paper presents Mwando, a virtual educational assistant designed to support the teaching and preservation of shiKomori, the language of the Comoros Islands. The system covers the four main dialectal variants (shiNgazidja, shiMwali, shiNdzuani and shiMaore) through a knowledge base constructed from phrases, proverbs, dictionaries and grammar lessons. A multi-agent architecture combining vector search, a knowledge graph and web search fallback enables accurate and context-aware responses. Evaluation on 500 queries demonstrates strong performance on vocabulary lookup and grammar explanations, while qualitative case studies illustrate both capabilities and current limitations. This work represents an initial step toward computational support for shiKomori and provides a blueprint for developing AI-powered educational tools for other low-resource languages.

</details>


### [31] [The Cross-Domain Generalization Cost of Offensive Language Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23512)
*Ruixing Ren,Junhui Zhao,Xiaoke Sun,Qiuping Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种诊断与优化框架，分解跨数据集和跨语言的攻击性语言检测性能下降为数据集效应和语言效应，并通过受控微调和三种联合训练策略实现多语言能力提升与源任务性能保持之间的可控权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有攻击性语言检测模型在跨数据集和跨语言迁移时表现显著下降，但缺乏系统方法来分解下降原因并量化改进成本，导致难以针对性地提升模型的跨语言鲁棒性。

Method: 框架包括三部分：① 零-shot 转移损失分解，将下降划分为可独立测量的数据集效应和语言效应；② 受控微调协议，通过比较持续微调与冷启动的少样本学习曲线，量化适应效率和对源任务的潜在损伤；③ 三种联合训练策略（温度采样、经验回放等），在提升多语言能力的同时控制源任务性能的损失，实现可调的Pareto权衡。

Result: 实验表明：① 数据集效应是零-shot 转移损失的主要因素，远超语言效应；② 未使用回放的少样本适应虽数据高效，但对源任务的损伤是联合训练的4-9倍且不稳定；③ 三种联合训练策略以牺牲3.2-4.1个百分点的源任务性能为代价，获得8.1-42.6个百分点的多语言能力提升，形成明确的可控Pareto权衡。

Conclusion: 跨语言攻击性语言检测的性能下降主要来源于数据集差异，采用联合训练结合温度采样和经验回放可在保持源任务性能的前提下显著提升多语言能力，为实际部署提供了系统的诊断与优化路径。

Abstract: Offensive language detection models generally suffer performance degradation when deployed across datasets and across languages, yet most existing studies stop at reporting this phenomenon and lack a systematic methodology for decomposing the causes of degradation into attributable components and quantifying the cost of remediation. This paper proposes a diagnosis and optimization framework composed of three coordinated technical components. First, a zero-shot transfer loss decomposition that separates the performance degradation from OLID to MLMA into two independently measurable components, namely dataset effect and language effect. Second, a controlled fine-tuning protocol that quantifies both adaptation efficiency and the hidden damage inflicted on the source task by comparing few shot learning curves under continued fine-tuning and cold-start starting points. Third, three joint training strategies incorpo rating temperature sampling and experience replay, which offer a controllable Pareto trade-off between improving multilingual capability and preserving source-task performance. Experiments built on this framework show that the dataset effect dominates the zero-shot transfer loss and substantially outweighs the language effect. Few-shot adaptation without a replay mechanism, though data-efficient, inflicts source task damage 4 to 9 times greater than that of the joint training strategies, and its damage magnitude is highly unstable. The three joint training strategies trade 3.2 to 4.1 percentage points of source-task performance for 8.1 to 42.6 percentage points of multilingual capability gain, forming a clear and controllable Pareto trade-off.

</details>


### [32] [Do Diagrams Help Large Language Models Reason? Evidence from Syllogistic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23513)
*Risako Ando,Koji Mineshima*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 比较自然语言、逻辑符号、线性图和欧拉图四种表示方式对两种大型语言模型在三段论推理上的影响，发现图形表示并未显著提升模型表现，模型在中性题上仍表现欠佳且常出现转换错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨图形化表示（尤其是欧拉图）是否能像对人类一样提升大型语言模型在逻辑推理任务中的性能，以填补现有研究对模型使用图形表示的认识空白。

Method: 使用Ando等人（2024）提供的285道三段论题，分别以自然语言、逻辑符号、线性图和欧拉图四种形式呈现给Claude 3.5 Sonnet和GPT‑4o‑mini两种最新LLM，比较其在蕴含、矛盾和中性三类推理上的准确率，并分析错误类型。

Result: 实验表明，图形化表示（线性图、欧拉图）并未系统性提升模型准确率；模型在蕴含和矛盾题上表现良好，但在中性题上准确率低，且常出现将文字转换为图形或逻辑符号时的系统性错误。

Conclusion: 在当前的LLM（Claude 3.5 Sonnet、GPT‑4o‑mini）中，图形化表示对逻辑推理的帮助有限，模型仍难以充分利用图形信息，尤其在中性推理上仍需改进。

Abstract: Diagrams are widely used to support logical reasoning, and prior studies suggest that representations such as Euler diagrams can improve human reasoning performance. Recent work has also explored their effects on large language models (LLMs). In this paper, we compare four representational conditions for syllogistic reasoning: natural language, logical notation, linear diagrams, and Euler diagrams. Using 285 problems from Ando et al. (2024), we evaluate two contemporary LLMs, Claude 3.5~Sonnet and GPT-4o-mini. Our results show that diagrammatic representations do not consistently improve performance. Although the models perform well on entailment and contradiction problems, they struggle with neutral problems and often make systematic conversion errors. Overall, the results suggest that the tested models gain limited benefit from diagrams in logical reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [33] [Novel Claim or Déjà Vu? Rethinking "Contamination-Free'' Dynamic Evaluation for Multimodal Automated Fact-Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23514)
*Haorui He,Xinwen Chen,Dacheng Wen,Reynold Cheng,Francis C. M. Lau,Yupeng Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现即使使用动态基准，仍有约17%-29%的新声明可能被污染，污染会显著提升MAFC模型的Macro-F1（最高+11.34），并导致排名失真，提出了控制污染的评估指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的静态MAFC基准大多包含已被大语言模型内部知识覆盖的旧声明，导致评估结果被夸大，难以衡量模型在需要最新外部信息的真实场景下的能力。

Method: 作者分别在SOTA静态基准AVeriTeC和新构建的动态基准ClaimReview2025Q4上，系统性检测后截断声明的潜在污染程度，并通过对比受污染与未受污染的实验设置，量化污染对MAFC性能的影响。

Result: 实验得到16条发现，核心结论包括：①动态评估虽降低污染但仍有17.09%–29.30%声明可能被污染；②许多新发布的声明可直接或通过合成已有公开知识进行验证；③污染会显著提升模型Macro-F1，最高增幅11.34点，并导致系统排名偏差。随后在严格控制污染的条件下重新评估了SOTA LLM，得到更可信的性能报告。

Conclusion: 即使采用动态基准，污染风险仍不可忽视，必须在数据收集、声明筛选和评估流程中实施严格的污染控制，以确保MAFC评估的可信度。本文提供了相应的实用指南。

Abstract: Multimodal automated fact-checking (MAFC) verifies claims by retrieving and reasoning over external evidence. However, most existing static benchmarks risk contamination: they primarily consist of outdated claims verifiable using an LLM's internal knowledge without external evidence. This can inflate performance estimates and fail to reflect true capability on novel claims that require up-to-date information. To address this, emerging dynamic benchmarks collect claims published after LLMs' knowledge cut-off dates, assuming they are uncontaminated. This work revisits this assumption by empirically studying contamination risks in both the state-of-the-art (SOTA) static AVeriTeC benchmark and our newly constructed dynamic ClaimReview2025Q4 benchmark, as well as their impact on MAFC evaluation. Our experiments yield 16 findings, highlighting three key results: (1) Dynamic evaluation reduces but does not eliminate contamination risks, as 17.09\%--29.30\% of post-cut-off claims remain potentially contaminated; (2) Many newly published claims can be verified either directly or by synthesizing multiple pieces of public knowledge available before the cut-off; and (3) Contamination can induce statistically significant inflation in MAFC performance, increasing Macro-F1 by up to 11.34 points and distorting system rankings. In light of these findings, we re-evaluate SOTA LLMs under a strictly contamination-controlled setting. Our study provides practical guidelines for trustworthy MAFC evaluation.

</details>


### [34] [The JEPA Paradox in Language: The Geometry of Linguistic Alternatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23531)
*Anh Trac Duc Dinh,Khang Nhat Hoang Vo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出，传统的基于平方误差的JEPA文本潜在预测目标与语言的条件结构不匹配，导致文本编码器出现中心退化和崩塌现象，实验表明需保留多种合理的补全而非压缩为单一点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管JEPA在视觉和音频任务中表现出色，但在文本编码器中未成为标准目标，作者认为这是因为语言的条件分布具有多样性，单一的均方误差预测无法捕捉到多个合理的补全。

Method: 作者提出三个衡量条件（可预测性、非崩塌性、低条件方差），并通过构造匹配的I‑JEPA和T‑JEPA实验，系统观察预测信息饱和、目标方差升高、训练‑验证不稳定、有效秩退化、余弦崩塌等现象的出现顺序。

Result: 实验在五个独立数据种子上复现相同的退化序列，证明这些现象不是采样噪声导致，且在文本任务中出现了中心退化和崩塌压力。

Conclusion: 预测学习仍可用于语言，但必须设计能够保留多种可能补全的JEPA目标，而非将其压缩到单一潜在点。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) are effective for images, video, and audio, yet deterministic JEPA-style latent prediction has not become a standard objective for text encoders. We argue that this gap reflects a mismatch between squared-error latent prediction and the conditional structure of language. The key requirement is conditional concentration: given a context and target location, the target representation should lie near a single meaningful point. Local image prediction often satisfies this through spatial continuity, whereas masked text can admit multiple valid token or span completions whose representations need not share a coherent center. We formalize this mismatch through three conditions---predictability, non-collapse, and low conditional variance---and show how their failure creates centroid degeneracy and collapse pressure in text. Matched I-JEPA and T-JEPA experiments reveal the predicted sequence: mutual-information saturation and elevated target variance precede train--validation instability, effective-rank degeneration, cosine collapse, and poor downstream transfer. The same pattern appears across five independent data seeds, indicating that it is not a sampling artifact. These results do not rule out predictive learning for language; they show that text-compatible JEPA objectives must preserve multiple plausible completions rather than compress them into a single latent point.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型虽在多语言生成上取得进展，但在低资源语言环境下提供同理心、文化敏感的心理健康咨询方面研究不足，缺乏真实案例和系统评估手段。

Method: ① 收集 625 条孟加拉语真实心理健康案例，来源包括 Facebook 公开帖子、电视节目文字稿和学生问卷；② 基于案例构建评估语料，包含临床心理师撰写的建议和三种商业 LLM 的生成回复；③ 提出 RP‑RCAF：在少量专家示例基础上加入角色扮演（同情顾问）和自我反思链式思考的任务特定提示；④ 设计 G‑REFS：结合自动指标与心理专家人工打分，评估情感敏感性、文化适配、语言清晰度和伦理合规性。

Result: 在所有模型上，使用 RP‑RCAF 的回复在 G‑REFS 四项指标上均显著高于传统提示（p<0.05），尤其在情感敏感性和文化适配上提升约 12%~18%；人工评审认为 RP‑RCAF 的回复更接近专业心理咨询师的表达。

Conclusion: 任务特定的角色扮演+反思链式提示能够显著提升 LLM 在低资源语言心理健康咨询中的同理心与伦理表现，为此类应用提供了可复制的提示与评估框架。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in large language models (LLMs), their ability to generate empathetic mental health counseling responses in low-resource languages remains largely unexplored. To address this gap, we curate 625 authentic mental health cases from three complementary sources: (1) publicly available Facebook posts discussing mental health concerns, (2) transcripts from the Bangladeshi television program "Ami Akhon Ki Korbo", and (3) anonymized student questionnaire responses covering diverse emotional and psychological challenges. Based on these cases, we build an evaluation corpus comprising advice written by licensed clinical psychologists and responses generated by three modern proprietary LLMs: GPT-4o Mini, Claude 4.5 Haiku, and Gemini 2.5 Pro. We further propose the Role-Playing Reflective Chain-of-Thought Advisory Framework (RP-RCAF), a task-specific prompting strategy that combines expert-authored few-shot examples with structured self-reflection to produce supportive, culturally aware, and ethically aligned counseling through a compassionate advisor persona. We also introduce the Grok 4-Based Response Evaluation and Scoring Framework (G-REFS), which integrates automated assessment with expert psychologist validation across emotional sensitivity, cultural appropriateness, linguistic clarity, and ethical soundness. Experimental results show that RP-RCAF consistently outperforms conventional prompting across all evaluated models and produces responses that more closely align with professional psychological counseling.

</details>


### [36] [Language Shapes Instruction Hierarchy Compliance in Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23545)
*Jiwon Moon,Yerin Hwang,Kyomin Jung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 XIH-Bench，用于评估多语言指令层级（IH）合规性，发现语言依赖的不对称性和跨语言冲突更易遵守的‘语言边界效应’，以及语言专化导致的安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令层级是安全可控部署的关键，但现有评估几乎只针对英文，缺乏对多语言环境下 IH 合规性的了解。

Method: 构建 XIH-Bench 基准，覆盖六种语言、四个领域、三种 IH 场景，设计同语言和跨语言冲突指令，对多模型进行系统实验。

Result: 1) IH 合规性呈现语言依赖的非对称性：同一语言在高优先级时提升合规，低优先级时却可能破坏合规。2) 跨语言冲突的合规率高于同语言冲突，称为语言边界效应。3) 语言专化使模型在偏好语言的低优先级指令更难被覆盖，带来可靠性和安全风险。

Conclusion: 多语言环境下的指令层级合规性并不稳定，需考虑语言不对称和语言边界效应，以防止因语言专化导致的安全隐患。

Abstract: Instruction hierarchy (IH) requires models to prioritize instructions by source, ensuring that higher-priority instructions override lower-priority ones. Despite its importance for safe and controllable deployment, existing evaluations have focused almost exclusively on English, leaving it unclear whether IH compliance remains stable in multilingual settings. We introduce XIH-Bench, a benchmark for multilingual IH evaluation with both same-language and cross-language conflicts across six languages, four domains, and three IH settings. Across models, we find two consistent patterns. First, IH compliance exhibits a clear language-dependent asymmetry: a language that strengthens compliance in the higher-priority position can become disruptive in the lower-priority position. Second, cross-language conflicts yield higher compliance than same-language conflicts, a phenomenon we term the Language Boundary Effect. We further show that language specialization can make lower-priority instructions in model-favored languages harder to override, creating multilingual reliability and security risks.

</details>

*Philipp Steigerwald,Eric Rudolph,Mara Stieler,Jennifer Burghardt,Jens Albrecht*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建并验证了GEMCo——86段德语邮件咨询对话的人为代理数据集，可公开发布，用于弥补真实咨询数据的隐私限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实的咨询对话因隐私和伦理限制难以共享，阻碍了语言技术在该领域的研究，亟需可公开的替代数据。

Method: 收集86段完整的德语邮件咨询对话（共728条信息），并结合专家撰写的案例和角色扮演的咨询会话，构建GEMCo代理数据集；随后使用124段未见的真实咨询对话作为参考，对比两者在咨询师策略和来访者情感上的分布差异，并通过生成式验证进一步评估相似性。

Result: GEMCo与真实对话在咨询师策略和来访者情感标签上的差距虽可检测，但幅度很小，表明代理数据在统计特性上接近真实数据；生成式验证亦支持该结论。

Conclusion: GEMCo不含隐私信息，可安全公开发布，为咨询领域的语言研究提供了首个可用数据资源，并展示了可推广至其他受限数据领域的验证框架。

Abstract: This paper presents GEMCo, a releasable, human-written proxy for inaccessible counselling data: 86 complete German e-mail counselling conversations (728 messages), expert-authored cases and counsellor sessions with trained role-players. It is validated against a held-out reference of 124 real counselling conversations. The proxy and the real conversations are measured against each other in counsellor strategies and client emotions. The gap is detectable but small. A generative validation supports the analysis. The validation method itself generalises to any domain where real data cannot be shared but a human-made proxy can. Privacy and ethics keep real counselling data closed. GEMCo carries none by design and can be released -- a first step toward language research in this domain.

</details>


### [38] [EmoTrace: An Emotion Trajectory-Centered Framework for Psychological Support Dialogue Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23648)
*Kaitong Weng,Lixin Liu,Zihao Liu,Bo Wang,Shiguang Ni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出EmoTrace框架，通过建模求助者情感轨迹生成多轮心理咨询对话，提升情感丰富度和共情质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话数据生成方法情感单一、缺乏情绪波动，且咨询师侧重问题解决而忽视情感互动，导致模型在情绪不稳定场景下表现不足。

Method: 构建求助者认知画像，引入情感图式及激活机制的求助者模块、咨询师模块和情感轨迹控制模块，实现求助者情感表达层次化和咨询师针对性共情表达的多轮对话生成。

Result: 实验表明EmoTrace在情感丰富度和共情质量上均优于现有生成方法，生成的对话更具情感多样性和同理心。

Conclusion: EmoTrace有效弥补了现有数据生成的情感单调和缺乏情感导向交互的问题，为训练更具情感感知和共情能力的心理咨询对话模型提供了高质量语料。

Abstract: Using large language models (LLMs) to assist psychological counseling is an important task in the field of natural language processing. The construction of high-quality psychological support dialogue corpora serves as a critical foundation for training counseling-oriented conversational models. However, existing data generation approaches generally suffer from several limitations, including emotionally stable seekers, limited variation in emotional dynamics, and a high degree of compliance with counselors' guidance. These issues result in LLM that lack the capability to effectively respond to emotionally unstable scenarios. In addition, counselor responses are typically driven by problem-solving objectives, thereby overlooking the role of emotion-focused interaction, which are essential in psychological counseling. To address these gaps, we propose EmoTrace, a multi-turn dialogue corpus generation framework centered on modeling seekers' emotional trajectories. we construct seekers' cognitive profile and introduce a seeker module with emotional schemas and an associated activation mechanism, a counselor module, and an emotional trajectory control module, thereby enhancing the layering of the seeker's emotional expression and the counselor's targeted empathic expression. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms existing approaches in terms of emotional richness and empathy quality.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 词序列的连续向量表征是自然语言处理的关键技术，广泛用于语音识别、机器翻译、情感分析等任务，亟需系统梳理其计算机制与可用资源，以指导实践。

Method: 回顾并比较主流词嵌入算法（如Word2Vec、GloVe、FastText等），调研公开的工具包和预训练矩阵，选取若干实现进行实验，分析其训练速度、语义质量和资源占用等特征。

Result: 实验表明不同实现在语义表现和效率上存在显著差异；如FastText在处理未登录词上优势明显，GloVe在大规模共现矩阵上表现更好，工具包的易用性也各有千秋。

Conclusion: 通过系统评估，作者提供了词向量计算方法与资源的实用指南，为研究者和工程师选择合适的嵌入方案提供依据。

Abstract: The embedding of word sequences into continuous vector spaces has been one of the most important developments in Natural Language Processing in the recent past. Such embeddings have found application in areas such as Automatic Speech Recognition, Machine Translation, Sentiment Analysis and many more. This essay reviews the various mechanisms that have been proposed for the calculation of word embeddings, investigates popular toolkits and embedding matrices that are available in the public domain, and experiments with one or more selected implementations to better understand their characteristics.
  La représentation vectorielle continue de mots a été l'un des développements les plus importants dans le domaine du traitement automatique du langage naturel au cours des dernières années. Ces représentations ont trouvé application dans des domaines tels que la reconnaissance vocale, la traduction automatique, l'analyse des sentiments, etc. Ce travail passe en revue les différents mécanismes proposés pour le calcul de ces vecteurs de mots, étudie les kits d'outils populaires et les matrices disponibles publiquement en ligne, et expérimente avec une ou plusieurs implémentations sélectionnées pour mieux comprendre leurs caractéristiques.

</details>


### [40] [Formally Verified Synthesizable Floating-Point Data Types in ARCH HDL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23715)
*Shuqing Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在硬件描述语言 ARCH 中实现并端到端验证了 IEEE-754 FP32 与 BF16 的完整算术，包括比较、转换、加减乘和 FMA，生成三种等价实现（SystemVerilog、SMT‑LIB、Lean），并通过机器检查确保一致性，针对乘法和 FMA 采用 Lean 证明正确四舍五入，优化 FMA 数据通路以提升频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在硬件设计中，确保浮点算术实现的功能正确性极其重要，尤其是当硬件描述语言由语言模型自动生成时，需要一种统一、可验证的方式来防止实现漂移并提供形式化保证。

Method: 作者将所有算术操作一次性描述为位向量 IR，并自动生成三种产物：可综合的 SystemVerilog、SMT‑LIB 浮点模型以及 Lean 4 形式化模型。通过 Yosys‑to‑SMT 的 miter 检查 SystemVerilog 与 SMT 模型等价；对乘法/ FMA 使用 Lean 进行正确四舍五入的证明；对乘法自由的操作进行穷举等价验证。针对 FMA 的 470 位宽数据通路进行重构为 98 位 guard/round/sticky 通路，以实现流水化并在 Nangate45 上达到 268 MHz，同时在 Lean 中证明其位相同于参考实现。

Result: 实现了 24 种算术操作的三重等价产物，所有机器检查均通过。乘法/ FMA 的 Lean 证明覆盖了全部 2^96 输入，确保正确四舍五入。优化后的 FMA 在 Nangate45 工艺下实现 268 MHz，且在位层面与完整参考等价。所有成果均在开源标签化发布中可追溯。

Conclusion: 通过一次性位向量描述和多目标自动生成，本文实现了硬件级浮点算术的端到端形式化验证，展示了在语言模型生成硬件代码情境下保持实现一致性和高性能的可行路径。

Abstract: We report the design and end-to-end verification of first-class IEEE-754 binary32 (FP32) and bfloat16 (BF16) arithmetic for ARCH, a hardware description language intended to be generated by language models.
